- [~] [L39](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/app.h#L39) `class wxAppConsole : public wxEvtHandler, public wxEventFilter {` — **Partial**: `call-with-application`, `run`, `stop`, `process-events`, `main-loop-running-p`, `pending-events-p`, `process-pending-events`, `application-handler`, `current-application-handler`; ABI: `wxl_start`, `wxl_run`, `wxl_stop`, `wxl_poll`, `wxl_main_loop_running`, `wxl_pending_events`, `wxl_process_pending_events`, `wxl_application_handler`. Managed bridge application only: session lifetime, native main loop, polling, queued-event status/dispatch and callback/pump guards. No constructible application wrapper, application traits, arbitrary event loops, command-line hooks or native virtual overrides. Arbitrary native event classes and persistent mutable events, pushed-handler ownership, filters, propagation, idle handlers, timers and worker dispatch remain open. Borrowed managed application handler view supports inherited binding/dispatch/state; construction and explicit deletion reject. OS session delivery and native application subclass hooks remain unverified/unbound. Query/end-session binding is available on the managed app and isolated owned handlers; synthetic app/window session emission rejects; OS delivery remains unverified.

Exact binding identity for the supported supported command/close kinds. Self/peer unbinding is safe; active native callback object is kept alive until return. No arbitrary functor/handler signatures or userData.

- [~] [L2052](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/event.h#L2052) `class wxCommandEvent : public wxEvent {` — **Partial**: `event`, `register-event-type`, `queue-event`, `process-event`, `event-integer`; ABI: `wxl_register_event_type`, `wxl_bind`, `wxl_queue_event`, `wxl_process_event`. Built-in and registered command tags with copied string/integer payloads and scoped dispatch. Remaining native fields/mutators, arbitrary derived classes, persistent ownership/cloning and worker dispatch remain open.
- [~] [L2058](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/event.h#L2058) `wxCommandEvent(wxEventType commandEventType = wxEVT_NULL, int id = 0);` — **Partial**: `queue-event`, `process-event`; ABI: `wxl_queue_event`, `wxl_process_event`. Built-in and registered command tags, copied ID/text/integer. Arbitrary native subclasses and persistent event objects are not exposed.

- [~] [L3568](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/event.h#L3568) `class wxNotifyEvent : public wxCommandEvent {` — **Partial**: `register-event-type`, `bind-event`, `queue-event`, `process-event`, `event-family`, `event-vetoed-p`, `(setf event-vetoed-p)`, `event-integer`; ABI: `wxl_register_event_type`, `wxl_bind`, `wxl_queue_event`, `wxl_process_event`, `wxl_context_vetoed`, `wxl_context_set_vetoed`. Registered synthetic notify types, immutable payload/family snapshots and scoped Veto/Allow. No native control-specific notify subclasses, persistent native event ownership/cloning, validated OS session delivery or userData. See docs/NOTIFY-EVENTS.md.
- [~] [L3574](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/event.h#L3574) `wxNotifyEvent(wxEventType eventType = wxEVT_NULL, int id = 0);` — **Partial**: `queue-event`, `process-event`; ABI: `wxl_queue_event`, `wxl_process_event`. Registered custom notify tags only, copied ID/text/integer and optional initial veto. No native control-specific derived events or persistent event wrapper.
- [~] [L3582](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/event.h#L3582) `void Allow();` — **Partial**: `(setf event-vetoed-p)`; ABI: `wxl_context_set_vetoed`. Equivalent NIL assignment clears notify veto in the current context. Snapshots remain immutable.
- [~] [L3588](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/event.h#L3588) `bool IsAllowed() const;` — **Partial**: `event-vetoed-p`, `process-event`; ABI: `wxl_context_vetoed`, `wxl_process_event`. Equivalent NOT event-vetoed-p, or inverse final veto result from ProcessEvent. Supported notify family only.
- [~] [L3597](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/event.h#L3597) `void Veto();` — **Partial**: `(setf event-vetoed-p)`; ABI: `wxl_context_set_vetoed`. True assignment vetoes the current notify event. Close-event CanVeto restrictions remain independently checked.

- [~] [L5232](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/event.h#L5232) `wxEventType wxEVT_END_SESSION;` — **Partial**: `event-logging-off-p`, `bind-event`, `event-type`, `queue-event`, `process-event`; ABI: `wxl_bind`, `wxl_event_type`, `wxl_queue_event`, `wxl_process_event`, `wxl_context_logging_off`, `wxl_context_set_logging_off`. Supported close/session snapshots and scoped contexts, including checked veto and logging-off metadata. Native app bindings exist; real OS session delivery is unverified. Synthetic session queue/process requires isolated owned handlers and blocks wxApp fallback. No persistent event ownership or arbitrary close types.
- [~] [L5233](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/event.h#L5233) `wxEventType wxEVT_QUERY_END_SESSION;` — **Partial**: `event-logging-off-p`, `bind-event`, `event-type`, `queue-event`, `process-event`; ABI: `wxl_bind`, `wxl_event_type`, `wxl_queue_event`, `wxl_process_event`, `wxl_context_logging_off`, `wxl_context_set_logging_off`. Supported close/session snapshots and scoped contexts, including checked veto and logging-off metadata. Native app bindings exist; real OS session delivery is unverified. Synthetic session queue/process requires isolated owned handlers and blocks wxApp fallback. No persistent event ownership or arbitrary close types.
